3a1(1): dang thams cad las//brtson 'grus ma rungs ngam du gzung /rdo rje lcags kyu mda' mgu dang//rin chen nyi ma rgyal mtshan 'dzum//pad+ma ral gri 'khor lo gsung//las dang go 
3a2(1): cha 'jigs byed bcangs//thog ma tha ma med cing zhi//ma rungs khro bar bzod pa che//gnod sbyin srin po dam pa brtan//gzi chen dpa' bo dbang po che//u ma'i bdag po skye dgu'i mgon//gtogs 'dod
3a3(1): rgyal 'dod thub pa che//'jig rten skyong dang mkha' dang sa//'jig rten gsum dang khams gsum pa//'byung chen sems can don rab mdzad//thams cad gu lang tshangs pa dang//'khor dang mya ngan 'da'
3a4(1): dang rtag/yang dag 'tsho dang mchod pa che//sangs rgyas dag pa theg pa che//srid gsum rtag pa'ang de yin no//'jig rten gsum rgyal bde spyod dang//bde spyod mgon po rab 'dul mdzad//
3a5(1): rdo rje gtso dang sa rab mchog/ye shes pha rol phyin pa'i tshul//byang chub sems dpa' rnam par thar//spyod dang de bzhin gshegs pa kun//sangs rgyas don dang sangs rgyas thugs//byang chub kun dang
3a6(1): bla na med//rnam par snang mdzad rgyal dang mgon//rang byung gzungs dang dran pa dang//sems dpa' che dang phyag rgya che//ting 'dzin sangs rgyas 'phrin las mdzad//yang dag sangs rgyas kun bdag nyid/
3a7(1): sems can rtag don rtogs mdzad pa//sdong po chen po nag po che//'dod chags chen po bde ba che//thabs chen dang ni mchog chen mchog/kun gyi mchog dang gnas dbang po//bcom ldan 'das byang chub chen
3a8(1): po'i thugs//byang chub sems dpa' chen po kun tu bzang po ni//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi thugs la gnas so//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyis sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'di//dper na til
3b1(1): gyi gang bu bzhin du yongs su gang bar gyur to//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad 'dus te/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen (po)don thams cad grub pa zhes bya ba byang chub kyi sems snying por bzhugs 
3b2(1): pa gang na ba der gshegs te/byang chub sems dpa'i de la ched du longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku bstan nas/'di skad ces bka' stsal to//rigs kyi bu khyod kyis gang gi phyir//de bzhin gshegs pa thams
3b3(1): cad kyi de kho na nyid mngon par ma rtogs par dka' ba spyang pa thams cad la spro ba skyed cing/ji ltar bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par bya snyam/de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems
3b4(1): dpa' chen po don thams cad grub pa de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyis bskul bas mi g.yo ba'i ting nge 'dzin las bzhengs te/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad la phyag byas nas 'di skad ces gsol to//
3b5(1): bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnams de kho na nyid ni ji lta bu lags/ji ltar ni bsgrub par bgyi ba bka' stsal du gsol//de skad ces gsol pa dang/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyis gsung 
3b6(1): cig tu 'di skad ces bka' stsal te//rigs kyi bu rang gi sems la so sor rtogs shing mnyam bar zhog la/rang bzhin gyi grub pa'i sngags 'di ji dgar bzlas shing bsgrub par gyis shig/oM tsit+ta pra ti bi d+haM ka ro mi 
3b7(1): de nas byang chub sems dpa'i don thams cad grub pas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad la 'di skad ces gsol to/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin bshegs pa rnams bdag gi snying po na zla ba'i dkyil 'khor lta bur  
3b8(1): gda' ba bdag gis rtogs so//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu sems 'di ni rang bzhin gyis 'od gsal bas na/'di ni ji ltar sbyangs pa de lta bur 'gyur te/dper na ras 
4a1(1): @#//dkar po tshon gyis bsgyur ba bzhin no//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis rang bzhin gyis 'od gsal ba'i sems shes pa brgyas par bya ba'i phyir/oM bo d+hi tsit+taM
4a2(1): ud+pa ta yA mi/zhes bya ba rang bzhin gyis grub pa'i sngags 'dis yang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po de byang chub kyi sems bskyed par gyur to//de nas byang chub sems dpas yang de bzhin 
4a3(1): gshegs pa thams cad kyi bkas byang chub kyi sems bskyed nas 'di skad ces gsol to//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor lta bur mthong ba gang lags pa de ni zla ba'i dkyil 'khor nyid du mthong ngo//de bzhin gsheg+sa 
4a4(1): pa thams cad kyis bka' stsal pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi snying po sems bskyed pa kun tu bzang po khyod kyis mngon sum du gyur gyis//de legs par sgrubs shig/de bzhin gshegs pa 
4a5(1): thams cad kyi sems bskyed pa kun tu bzung po de brtan par bya ba'i phyir/sngags 'dis rang gi snying ka'i zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la rdo rje dbyibs su soms shig/oM tiSh+Tha badzra/byang chub sems  
4a6(1): dpas gsol ba/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnams zla ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi steng na rdo rjer bdag gis mthong ngo//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis bka' rtsal pa/de bzhin gsheg+sa 
4a7(1): pa thamasacad kyi kun tu bzang po'i sems rdo rje de sngags 'dis brtan par gyis shig/oM badzra Ad+ma ko d+ha haM/de nas nam mkha'i dbyings thams cad du mnyam par 'jug pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad 
4a8(1): kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje'i dbyings ji snyed pa de dag thams cad de bzhin gshegs pa thamscad kyi byin gyi brlabs kyis sems dpa' rdo rje der zhugs so//de nas de na bzhin gshegs pa thams 
4b1(1): cad kyis bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' chen po don thams cad grub pa de la rdo rje'i dbyings/rdo rje'i dbyings zhes bya ba'i rdo rje ming gi dbang bskur ro//de nas byang chub sems dpa' rdo rje dbying+sa
4b2(1): kyis de bzhin gshegs pa de dag thams cad la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnams de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku ni bdag legs par mthong ngo//
4b3(1): de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyis bka' stsal pa/sems dpa' chen po de lta bas na sems dpa' rdo rje de rnam pa thams cad kyi mchog dang ldan pa'i sangs rgyas kyi skur bdag nyid rang bzhin gyis 
4b4(1): grub pa'i sngags 'di ci dgar zlos la sgom sa shig/oM ya thA sarba ta thA ga ta sta thA a haM//de nas de skad ces bka' stsal pa dang/byang chub sems dpa' chen pa rdo rje dbyings kyis bdag nyid de bzhin gsheg+sa
4b5(1): par mngon bar rdzogs par sangs rgyas nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad la phyag byas te bos nas 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das de bzhinagashegs pa thams cad kyis bdag byin gyis
4b6(1): rlobs la mngon par rdzogs par byang chub pa de brtan par mdzod cig/de nas de skad ces gsol pa dang/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad de bzhin gshegs pa rdo rje dbyings kyi sems dpa' rdo rje der 
4b7(1): zhugs sa so//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rdo rjai dbyings kyis skad cig ma de nyid kyi tshe na/de bzhin gshegs pa thamams cad kyi mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes mngon par rdzogs par
4b8(1): sangs rgyas so//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis rdo rje mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes kyi phyag rgya gsang ba'i dam tshig tu zhugs so//de bzhin gshegasapa thams cad kyi chos mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes
6a1(1): @#//nyid dang/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis byin gyi brlabs kyis cig tu 'dres par gyur nas/nam mkha'i khams thams cad kyi tshad dang mnyam par 'jug pa'i 'od 
6a2(1): gzer gyi 'phreng ba can rtse mo lnga pa de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje'i rang bzhin gyi rdo rje'i dbyings su gyur nas/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi thugs ka nas byung ste//
6a3(1): phyag gnyis su gnas so//de nas rdo rje de las rdo rje lta bu'i 'od gzer gzugs dang kha dog sna tshogs pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du snang bas khyab par byed pa rnams byung ngo//rdo rje lta bu'i 'od gzer 
6a4(1): de dag gi rtse mo las 'jig rten gyi khams thamasacad kyi rdul shin tu phra mo snyed kyi de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi skyu byung ste/chos kyi dbyings kun du khyab pa thams cad dang nam mkha'i khams kyi mtha' gtugs 
6a5(1): pa thams cad dang 'jig rten gyi khams thamasas cad rab 'byam rgya mtsho'i sprin thams cad du/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi mnyam ba nyid kyi ye shes mngon par shes pa mngon par byang chub bas//
6a6(1): de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi byang chub kyi sems chen po bskyed pa dang/kun tu bzang po'i spyod pa sna tshogs sgrub pa dang/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi rigs mnyes par mdzad pa dang/byang chub kyi 
6a7(1): snying po chen por gshegs pa dang/bdud thams cad gdul ba dang/de bzhin gshegs thams cad kyi mnyam pa nyid kyi byang chub chen por mngon par byang chub pa dang/dam pa'i chos kyi 'khor lo bskor 
6a8(1): ba nas|ji srid sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa rnams yongs su skyob cing thams cad la phan pa dang/bde bar mdzad pa dang/de bzhin gshegasapa thams cad kyi ye shes dang//
6b1(1): mngon bar shes pa dam pa'i dngos grub sgrub pa la sogs pa de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi thugs kyi rdzu 'phrul dang/cho 'phrul rnams (kyi)bar du bstan nas/rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni kun tu bzang 
6b2(1): po nyid yin pa dang/shin tu brtan pa nyid yin pa'i phyir gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas/byang chub sems dpa' chen po kun tu bzang po'i skur gyur te/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs 
6b3(1): la gnas nas/ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//e ma'o bdag ni rang 'byung ba'i//kun tu bzang po'i sems dpa' brtan//de ni bstan phyir lus min yang//sems dpa'i lus su gyur 
6b4(1): ba yin;de nas byang chub sems dpa' chen pa kun tu bzang po'i sku bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs ka nas byung ste/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi spyan snga na zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas nas bka' 
6b5(1): nod cing bzhugs par gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi ye shes kyi dam tshig rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa 
6b6(1): thams cad kyi tshul khrims dang/ting nge 'dzin dang/shes rab dang/rnam par grol ba dang/rnam par grol ba'i ye shes mthong ba dang/chos gyi 'khor lo bskor ba dang/sems can gyi don bya ba 
6b7(1): dang ;thabs chen po dang/stobs dang/brtson 'grus dang/ye shes chen po'i dam tshig sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa yongs su bskyab pa dang/thams cad kyi dbang po(ra) 
6b8(1): bya ba dang/thams cad kyi bde ba dang/yid bde ba myong bar bya ba nas/ji srid de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes dang mngon par shes pa bla na med pa'i theg pa chen po 
7a1(1): @#//mngon par rtogs pa'i grub pa'i mchog thob pa'i bar du/'bras bu de dag gi phyir byang chub sems dpa' chen po kun tu bzang po de/de bzhin gshegs pa   
7a2(1): thams cad kyi 'khor lo sgyur ba nyid du de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku rin po che ni bang bu rgyan dang cod pan gyi dbang gyi dbang gis dbang bskur nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi grub 
7a3(1): pa'i rdo rje de de'i lag par byin no//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyis lag na rdo rje lag na rdo rje zhes rdo rje ming gi dbang gis dbang bskur ro//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' 
7a4(1): chen po phyag na rdo rje phyag g.yon pa rdo rje bsgyings par bzhugs/phyag g.yas pas rdo rje thugs kar gsor zhing 'phen pa'i tshul du 'dzin cing ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//sangs rgyas kun  
7a5(1): gyis grub pa yi//rdo rje bla na med pa 'di//bdag ste bdag gi lag tu byin//rdo rje la ni rdo rje gnas//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis yang byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po gdon mi za 
7a6(1): ba'i rgyal po'i dam tshig las byung ba'i sems dpa' byin gyis brlabs pa'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pathams cad spyan drang ba'i dam tshig ces bya ba 
7a7(1): de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi snying po 'di rang gi thugs ka nas phyung ngo//badzra rA dzA/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi thugs ka nas byung ma thag tu 'di ni/bcom ldan 'das phyag 
7a8(1): na rdo rje de nyid de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi lcags kyu chen por gyur te byung nas/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs su zhugs te/gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas//rdo rje lcags 
7b1(1): kyu chen po'i dbyibs su gyur te/bcom ldan 'das kyi phyag gnyis su gnas so//de nas rdo rje lcags kyu'i dbyibs chen po de las 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra mo dang mnyam pa'i de 
7b2(1): bzhin gshegs pa'i sku rnams byung ste/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad spyan drang ba la sogs pa sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rdzu 'phrul dang/cho 'phrul rnams byas nas/rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin 
7b3(1): ni shin tu gdon mi za bar rgyal po nyid yin pa dang/shin tu brtan pa'i phyir gcig tu 'dres par gyur te/byang chub sems dpa' chen po gdon mi za ba'i rgyal po'i lus su gyur nas/bcom ldan 
7b4(1): 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs la gnas te ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//e ma'o rdo rje las byung ba'i//lcags kyu nges pa'i rgyal po bdag/gang phyir kun tu sangs rgyas khyab//
7b5(1): grub phyir kun nas spyan 'dren no//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chen pa shin tu gdon mi za ba'i rgyal po' sku bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs ka nas byung ste/de bzhin gsheg pa thams cad kyi 
7b6(1): g.yas phyogs kyi zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas nas/yang bka' nod cing bzhugs par gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad spyan 'dren pa'i dam tshig 
7b7(1): gi rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad spyan 'dren pa'i dam tshigs dang/sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa thams cad 
7b8(1): dgug pa dang/thams cad kyi bde ba dang yid bde ba myong bar bya ba nas/de bzhin gshegs thams cad 'du ba'i byin gyis brlabs dam pa'i dngos grub gyi bar gyi phyir//rdo rje lcags kyu de 
8a1(1): @#//byang chub sems dpa'i gdon mi za ba'i rgyal po de'i lag par de bzhin du byin no//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis rdo rje 'gugs pa rdo rje 'gugs pa zhes bya ba'i 
8a2(1): dbang gi dbang bskur ro//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rdo rje 'gugs pa rdo rje'i lcags kyu des de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad spyan 'dren cing ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//sangs rgyas 
8a3(1): kun gyi rdo rje yi//ye shes bla na med pa 'di/sangs rgyas kun gyi don grub phyir//kun nas spyan 'dren dam pa'o//de nas yang bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' chen po bdud ces bya ba'i dam tshig las 
8a4(1): byung ba'i sems pa'i byin gyis brlabs rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad mnyes par byed pa'i dam tshig ces bya ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa thams
8a5(1): cad kyi snying po 'di rang gi thugs ka nas phyung ngo//badzra rA ga/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi thugs las byung ma thag pa 'di ni bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'dzin pa de nyid te//de bzhin gshegs pa 
8a6(1): thams cad kyi me tog gi mngar gyur nas byung ste/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs su zhugs te//gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas/rdo rje'i mnga' chen po'i dbyibs su gyur te/phyag gnyis su gan(sa)
8a7(1): so|de nas rdo rje'i mnga' dbyibs de las 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra mo dang mnyam pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku rnams byung ste/de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad rjes su mnyes pa(ra)
8a8(1): bya ba la sogs pa dang/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rdzu 'phrul (dpe/chos srulo )rnams byas nas/rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin shin tu bdud byed pa dang/rab tu brtan pa'i phyir gcig tu 'dres par gyur na/byang chub sem(sa)
8b1(1): dpa' chen po bdud ces bya bar gyur te/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad gyi thugs la gnas nas mdzad ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//e ma'o bdag gi rang bzhin dag/rang 'byung ba dag dgyes byed na//
8b2(1): 'dod chags bral rnams dag bya'i phyir/'dod chags kyis ni 'dul bar byed//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chen po bdud de'i lus bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs ka nas byung ste/de bzhin gshegs pa thams
8b3(1): cad kyi g.yon phyogs kyi zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas nas yang bka' nod cing bzhugs so//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad mnyes par bya ba'i byin gyis brlabs rdo rje zhes bya ba'i
8b4(1): ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi bdud rdo rje bi dam tshig dang/sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa rnams mgu bar bya ba dang/thams cad kyi
8b5(1): bde ba dang/yid bde ba nas/de bzhin gshegs thams cad kyi bdud kyi las kyi dngos grub kyi mchog thob pa'i bar du 'bras bu de dag gi phyir rdo rje'i mnga' de byang chub sems dpa' chenapo bdud ces bya ba de'i lag tu
8b6(1): de bzhin du byin no//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thamams cad la bdud byed cing/ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//kyis rdo rje'i gzhu zhes bya ba'i dbang gis dbang bskur ro//de nas byang chub sems dpa'
8b7(1): sems dpa' chen po rdo'i gzhu zhes bya bas/rdo rje'i mnga' des de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad la bdud byed cing /ched du brjod pa 'di tshed du brjod do//sangs rgyas kun gyi 'dod chags kyi//ye shes 'di
8b8(1): ni dri ma med//'dod chags kyi ni chags bral bcom//bde ba thams cad sbyin par byed//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis yang byang chub sems dpa' chen po rab tu dga' ba'i rgyal po'i dam tshig las byung ba'i
9a1(1): @#//sems dpa'i byin gyis brlabs rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rab tu dga' ba'i dam tshig ces bya ba de bzhin 
9a2(1): gshegs pa thams cad kyi snying po 'di rang gi thugs ka nas phyung ngo//badzra sA d+hu /de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi thugs las byung ma thag pa 'di ni bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'dzin pa de nyid de legs 
9a3(1): so zhes bya ba'i gyur nas /bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs su zhugs te/gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas/rdo rje dgyes pa'i dbyibs su gyur te/phyag gnyis su gnas so//de nas rdo rje dgyes pa'i 
9a4(1): dbyings de las 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra mo dang mnyam pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku rnams byung nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi legs so//zhes bya ba la sogs 
9a5(1): pa dang /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rdzu 'phrul dang cho 'phrul rnams byas nas rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni shin tu dga' bar byed pa dang /rab tu brtan pa'i phyir gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas//
9a6(1): byang chub sems dpa' rab tu dga' ba'i rgyal po'i lus su gyur te/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs la gnas ched du brjod pa 'di brjod do//e ma'o thams 
9a7(1): cad mkhyen kun gyi//legs byin nang na bdag mchog pas//rnam rtog bral ba de dag gi/nges par dgyes pa bskyed pa yin//de nas byang chub sems dpa' rab du dga' ba'i rgyal po'i sku 
9a8(1): de bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs ka nas byung ste/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rgyab na/zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas nas/yang bka'i nod cing bzhugs bar gyur to//de nas bcom 
9b1(1): ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad mnyes par byed pa'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad kyi rab tu dgyes pa bla na med pa'i ye shes kyi 
9b2(1): dam tshig sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa'i sems can thams cad mgu bar bya ba dang/thams cad kyi bde ba dang yid bde ba myong bar bya ba nas/de nas bzhin gshegs pa 
9b3(1): thams cad dgyes pa'i ro bla na med pa dam pa 'grub pa thob pa'i bar du 'bras bu de dag gi phyir rdo rje dgyes pa de byang chub sems dpa' chen po rab tu dga' ba'i rgyal po de'i lag tu de bzhin du byin no//
9b4(1): de nas de bzhin gshegs pa mthams cad kyis rdo rje dgyes pa rdo rje dgyes pa zhes rdo rje zhes bya ba'i dbang gis bskur ro//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rdo rje dgyes pas//
9b5(1): rdo rje dgyes pa'i dbyibs nges de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad la legs so zhes bya bas rab tu dgyes par byed cing ched du brjod do//sangs rgyas kun gyi legs byin pa/'di ni rab tu 'jug  
9b6(1): byed pas//rdo rje thams cad dgyes par byed pa'i lha rdzas dgyes pa spel ba'o////byang chub sems dpa' chen pa dang/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad spyan drang ba'i dam tshig dang//
9b7(1): de nas bzhin gshegs pa thams can mnyes par byed pa'i ye shes dang/dgyes pa chen po dang/'di bzhin ni de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi dam tshig chen po'i sems dpa'o//de nas yang 
9b8(1): bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' chen po nam mkha'i snying po'i dam tshig las byung ba rin po che'i byin gyi brlabs rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gsheg+sa 
10a1(1): @#//pa thams cad kyi dbang bskur ba'i dam tshig ces bya ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi snying po rang gi thugs ka nas phyung ngo//badzra rad+na/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams
10a2(1): cad kyi thugs las byung ma tham thag pa 'di ni rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin thams cad nam mkha' dang mnyam par nyid kyi ye shes shin tu rtogs pa yin pa'i phyir ro//bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'dzin pa de nyid de nam  
10a3(1): mkha' thams cad kyi 'od gzer du gyur nas byung ngo//nam mkha' thams cad kyi 'od zer de dag gis 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad snang bar byas pas thams cad nam mkha'i dbyings lta bur gyur to//
10a4(1): de nas de bzhin gshegs par thams cad kyi byin gyis brlabs kyis nam mkha'i dbyings de dag thams cad bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs su zhugs nas/rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge mdzin shin tu bsgom+sa 
10a5(1): par gyur pa'i phyir/yang nam mkha'i khams thams cad las byung ba 'jig rten gyi khams thamasacad du mnyam par 'jug pa'i tshad tsam rdo rje rin po che chen po'i dbyings su gyur nas/de bzhin gshegs pa'i phyag 
10a6(1): gnyis su gnas so//de nas rdo rje rin po che'i dbyings de las 'jig rten gyi khams tham sa cad kyi rdul shin tu phra mo dang mnyam ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi skur gyur nas//de bzhin gshegs pa thams 
10a7(1): cad kyi dbang la sogs dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rdzu 'phrul dang/cho 'phrul rnams 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du byas nas/rdo rje sems dpa' ting nge 'dzin ni nam mkha'i khams thams chad kyi snying po las shin
10a8(1): tu byung ba dang/rab tu brtan pa'i phyir gcig tu 'dres par gyur te/byang chub sems dpa' chen po nam mkha'i snying po'i skur gyur nas/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs la gnas nas/ched du brjod pa 'di ched 
10b1(1): du brjod do//e ma'o chags med rgyal ba yang/khams gsum dbang por dgongs par na//rdo rje rin chen dam pa yi//dbang gi mchog ni bdag yin no//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chen po nam mkha'i snying po'i 
10b2(1): lus de bcom ldan 'das kyis thugs nas byung ste/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad spyan snga na/zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas nas yang bka' nod cing bzhugs par gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de bzhin gshegs 
10b3(1): pa thams cad kyi nor bu rin po che rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi dgongs pa yongs su rdzogs par bya ba'i dam tshig dang/sems can gyi khams ma lus 
10b4(1): shing lus pa med pa'i don thams cad yongs su grub par bya ba dang/thams cad kyi bde ba dang yid bde ba myong bar bya ba nas/de bzhin gshegs par thams cad kyi don phun sum tshogs pa'i grub pa thob par 
10b5(1): bya ba'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' chenapo nam mkha'i snying po'i rdo rje rin po che chen po 'khor lo bskor bar/rdo rje cha rin po che myu gu'i dbang gis dbang bskur nas/lag tu rdo rje'i nor bu de byin no//de nas de 
10b6(1): bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis rdo rje snying po zhes rdo rje zhes bya ba'i dbang gis dbang bskur ro//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chen po rdo rje snying pos rdo rje'i nor bu de rang gi dbang bskur ba'i gnas su 'jog cing ched du brjod pa 
10b7(1): 'di ched du brjod do//sangs rgyas kun gyi sems gyi sems dpa'i dbyings//dbang bskur de ni bdag yin te//bdag gi lag tu byin pas na//rin chen la ni rin chen bzhag/de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis 
10b8(1): byang chub sems dpa' chenapa gzi brjid chen po zhes bya ba'i dam tshig las byung ba'i rin po che'i byin gyi brlabs rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams can kyi 'od gzer 
11a1(1): @#//gyi dam tshig ces bya ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi snying po 'di rang gi thugs ka nas phyung ngo//badzra te dza/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi thugs las phyung ma thag 
11a2(1): pa 'di ni bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rje de nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor chen por gyur nas/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs su zhugs te/gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas/rdo rje nyi ma'i dbyings su gyur te/
11a3(1): bcom ldan 'das kyi phyag gnyis su gnas so//de nas rdo rje nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor de las 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra mo dang mnyam pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa thabs rnams kyi sku byung 
11a4(1): nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi 'od gzer 'gyed pa la sogs pa dang/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi rdzu 'phrul rnams byas nas/rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni shin tu gzi brjid che ba nyid 
11a5(1): dang /shin tu brtan pa'i phyir gcig tu 'dres par gyur te/byang chub sems dpa' chen po gzi brjid chen po'i skur gyur nas/bcom ldan 'dis rnam par snang mdzad kyi thags la gnas te//
11a6(1): ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//e ma'o sems can khams kun du //snang byed gzi brjid dpe med bdag/gang phyir sangs rgyas dag pa yi//'dren pa rnams kyang sbyong bar byed//de nas 
11a7(1): byang chub sems chen po dri ma med pa'i gzi brjid de'i lus bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs las byung ste/de bzhin gshegs pa thamams cad kyi g.yas phyogs su zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas te//
11a8(1): bka' don da cing bzhugs par gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi 'od kyi dkyil 'khor gyi byin gyis brlabs rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs 
11b1(1): nas;de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi 'od gzer gyi dam tshig dang/sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa rnams kyi gzi brjid mtshungs pa med pa dang/thams cad kyi bde ba dang yid bde ba 
11b2(1): myong bar bya ba nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi rang bzhin gyi 'od 'thob cing dngos grub dam pa'i bar du 'bras bu de dag gi phyir/rdo rje nyi ma de byang chub sems dpa' chen po gzi brjid chen po 
11b3(1): de'i lag tu de bzhin du byin no//de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyis rdo rje zhes bya ba'i dbang gis dbang bskur ro//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chenapo rdo rje 'od rdo rje 'od nyi ma nges de bzhin  
11b4(1): gshegs pa thams cad la snang bar byed cing/ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//sangs rgyas kun gyi snang ba 'di//mi shes mun nag 'joms byed pa//shin tu phra rdul ji snying pa'i//nyi ma'i 'od pas shin 
11b5(1): tu lhag/de nas yang bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa'  chen po rine dpal gyi dam tshig las byung ba rin po che'i byin gyis brlabs rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de nas 
11b6(1): bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dgongs pa yongs su rdzogs par byed pa'i dam tshig zhes bya ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa thams can kyi snying po 'di rang gi thugs ka nas phyung ngo//badzra ke du/de nas de bzhin 
11b7(1): gshegs pa thams cad kyi thugs las phyung ma thag pa 'di ni/bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'dzin pa de nyid de/rgyal mtshan kha dog dang gzugs dang rgyan dang dbyibs sna tshogs par gyur te/byung nas bcom ldan 
11b8(1): 'das rnam par sna mdzad kyi thugs su zhugs te gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas/rdo rje rgyal mtshan gyi dbyibs su gyur te/phyag gnyis su gnas so//de nas rdo rje rgyal mtshan gyi dbyibs/de las 'jig rten gyi 
12a1(1): nya                khams thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra mo dang mnyam pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku rnams byung nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rin po che'i rgyal mtshan bsgreng ba la sogs pa 
12a2(1): sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rdzu 'phrul dang cho 'phrul rnams byas nas/rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni rin po che chen po'i dpal dang shin tu brtan pa yin pa'i phyir gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas/byang chub sems dpa' 
12a3(1): chen po rin po che dpal gyi skur gyur nas/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs la gnas nas/ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//e ma'o don kun grub pa dang//dgongs pa thams cad rdzogs 
12a4(1): pa yi;;don rnams thams cad rdzogs byed pa'i//'dra med dpal ni bdag yin no//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chen po rin po che'i dpal gyi sku bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs ka nas byung ste/de bzhin gshegs 
12a5(1): pa thams cad kyi g.yon gyi phyogs kyi zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas nas yang bka' don cing bzhugs par gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rgyal mtshan gyi byin gyi rlabs rdo rje 
12a6(1): zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis nor bu ying bzhin gyi rgyal mtshan bsgreng ba'i dam tshig dang/sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa rnams kyi re 
12a7(1): ba thams cad yongs su bskong bar bya ba dang thams cad kyi bde dang yid bde ba myong bar bya ba nas/de bzhin gshegs pathams cad kyi don chen po'i dngos grub dam pa thob pa'i bar du 'bras bu de dag gi phyir//
12a8(1): rdo rje'i rgyal mtshan de byang chub sems dpa' chen po rin po che'i dpal de'i lag du de bzhin du byin no//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyis rdo rje rgyal mtshan rdo rje rgyal mtshan zhes bya ba ni rdo rje ming gi dbang gis 
12b1(1): dbang bskur ro//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rdo rje rgyal mtshan gyis/rdo rje rgyal mtshan nges de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa la sbyor du bcug nas//ched du 
12b2(1): brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//sangs rgyas kun gyi dgongs pa kun//yongs su rdzogs par byed pa yi//yid bzhin nor bu rgyal mtshan 'di//sbyin pa'i pha rol phyin pa'i tshul//de nas bcom 
12b3(1): ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' chen po rtag par dga' zhing rab tu mgu ba'i dbang po'i dam tshig las byung ba rin po che'i byin byi brlabs rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/
12b4(1): de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi dgyes pa'i dam tshig zhes bya ba/de bzhin gshegs pa thams thams cad kyi snying po 'di rang gi thugs ka nas phyung ngo//badzra hA sa/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad 
12b5(1): kyi thugs las byung ma thag pa 'di ni//bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'dzin pa de nyid de de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi 'dzum par gyur te/byung nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs su 
12b6(1): zhugs te/gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas/rdo rje 'dzum pa'i dbyibs su gyur te phyag gnyis su gnas so//de nas rdo rje 'dzum pa'i dbyibs de las/'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rdul shin 
12b7(1): tu phra ba dang mnyam pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku rnams byung nas//de bzhin gshegs pa thams zad kyi rmad du byung ba la sogs pa dang/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rdzu 'phrul dang /cho 'phrul 
12b8(1): rnams byas nas/rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni rtag tu dga' zhing rab tu mgu ba'i dbang po yin pa dang shin tu brtan pa'i phyir gcig tu 'dres par gyur na//byang chub sems dpa' chen po 
13a1(1): @#//rtag tu dga' zhing rab tu mgu ba'i dbang po'i lus su gyur nas/bcom ldan 'das rnams par snang mdzad kyi thugs la gnas te/ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//rtag 
13a2(1): tu mnyam par gzhag rnams kyi//sangs rgyas don la rab sbyor phyir//e ma'o bdag ni kun mchog gi/bzhad chen rmad byung chen po yin//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chen po rtag par dga' zhing rab tu mgu
13a3(1): ba'i dbang po'i lus bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs las byung nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rgyab kyi zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas nas/yang bka' nod cing bzhugs par gyur to//
13a4(1): de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi rmad du byung ba'i byin gyi brlabs zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs thams cad kyi rmad du 
13a5(1): byung ba bskyed pa'i dam tshig dang/sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa thams cad kyi dbang po bla na med pa thams cad bde ba dang/yid bde ba myong bar bya ba nas/de bzhin  
13a6(1): gshegs pa thams cad kyi dbang bo yongs su sbyong ba'i ye shes mngon bar shes pa'i bar du 'bras bu de dag gi phyir/byang chub sems dpa' chen pi rtag par dga' zhing rabatu mgu ba'i dbang po de'i lag tu rdo 
13a7(1): rje 'dzum pa de de bzhin du byin no//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyis rdo rje dga' ba rdo rje dga' ba zhes rdo rje ming gi dbang gis dbang bskur ro//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen 
13a8(1): po rdo rje dga' bas/rdo rje 'dzum pa des de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dgyes par byed cing /ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//'di de sangs rgyas thams cad kyi/rmad du 'byung bskyed ston 
13b1(1): pa po;;dgyes chen byed pa'i ye shes te//gtsug lag gzhan gyis shes mi nus//dbang bskur ba ni chen po dang//'od kyi dkyil 'khor 'dom gang dang/sems dpa' chen po'i don
13b2(1): dang ;dgyes pa chen po dang 'di bzhin de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dbang bskur ba chen po'i sems dpa'o//de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' chen po 
13b3(1): kun du spyan ras gzigs kyi dbang po'i dam tshig las byung ba'i chos kyi byin gyi brlabs rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi chos 
13b4(1): kyi dam tshig zhes bya ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi snying po 'di rang gi thugs ka nas phyung ngo//badzra d+har+m+ma/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thamscad kyi thugs las byung ma thag pa 
13b5(1): 'di ni bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'dzin pa de nyid de /rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni rang bzhin gyis dag pa'i chos mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes rab tu rtogs pa'i phyir/dam pa'i chos kyi 'od gzer du gyur 
13b6(1): te byung ngo//dam pa'i chos kyi 'od gzerade dag gis 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du kun tu snang bar byas nas chos kyi rang bzhin du gyur to//chos kyi dbyings de dag thams cad kyang bcom ldan 'das 
13b7(1): rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs su zhugs te gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas/nam mkha'i khams thams cad dang mnyam bar 'jug pa'i tshad tsam gyis rdo rje pad+ma chen po'i dbyibs su gyur te/phyag gnyis
13b8(1): su gnas so//de nas rdo rje pad+ma'i dbyibs de las 'jig rten gyi khams tham sa cad kyi rdul phra mo dang mnyam pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku rnams byung ste/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi ting 
14a1(1): @#//nge 'dzin gyi ye shes dang mngon bar shes pa la sogs par dang/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rdzu 'phrul dang cho 'phrul rnams 'jig rten gyis khams 
14a2(1): thams cad du byas nas/rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni shin tu kun tu spyan ras gzigs kyi dbang po yin ba dang shin tu brtan pa'i phyir/gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas//
14a3(1): byang chub sems dpa' chen po kun tu spyan ras gzigs ki dbang po'i skur gyur te/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs par snang mdzad kyi thugs la gnas nas//ched du brjod 
14a4(1): pa 'di ched du brjod do//e ma'o rang 'byung gdod nas dag/dam pa'i don ni bdag yin te//gang phyir gzings 'dra'i chos rnams las//rnam par dag pa dmigs par gyur//
14a5(1): de nas byang chub sems dpa' chen bo kun tu spyan ras gzigs ku lus de bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs 'dra'i chos rnams las byung ste/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi spyan 
14a6(1): snga na zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas te/bka' nod cing bzhugs par gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin bshegs pa thams cad kyi ting nge 'dzin gyi mnyam ba nyid ye shes 
14a7(1): dam tshig las byung ba rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa tha mas ma bcad rnam par sbyang ba'i dam tshig dang/sems can gyi khams ma lus shing 
14a8(1): lus pa med pa rnams kyis bdag dang gzhan rnam par dag par bya ba dang /thams cad kyi bde ba dang/yid bde ba myong bar bya ba nas/de bzhin gshegs thams cad kyi chos kyi ye shes 
14b1(1): dang mngon par shes pa drug thob pa'i bar du 'bras bu de dag gi phyir/byang chub sems dpa' chen po kun tu spyan ras gzigs kyi dbang po dam pa'i chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba nyid dang/de bzhin 
14b2(1): gshegs pa thams cad kyi chos kyi sku'i dbang gis dbang bskur nas/de'i lag tu rdo rje pad+mo de byin no//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis rdo rje'i spyan rdo rje'i spyan zhes rdo rje ming gi dbang gis 
14b3(1): dbang bskur ro//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rdo rje spyan gyis rdo rje'i pad+mo'i 'dab ma de kha 'phyed pa('a)di da/'dod chags rnam par dag cing ma chags pa'i ngo bo nyid du gzigs pas 
14b4(1): gzigs shing ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod//sangs rgyas kun gyi 'dod chags kyis//de yi kho nas rtogs pa 'di//bdag ste bdag gi lag du byin//chos la chos ni gnas par gyur//de nas 
14b5(1): yang bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' chen pa 'jam dpal gyi dam tshig las byung ba'i chos kyi byin gyis brlabs rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin 
14b6(1): gshegs pa thams cad kyi shes rab dang ye shes chen po'i dam tshig ces bya ba/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi snying po 'di rang gi thugs ka nas byung ngo//badzra tik+Sh+N+Sha/de nas de bzhin gshegs 
14b7(1): pa thams cad kyi thugs las byung ma thag pa 'di ni bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'dzin pa de nyid de shes rab kyi ye shes kyi ral grir gyur te/byung nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs su zhugs 
14b8(1): te gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas/rdo rje ral gri'i dbyibs su gyur te/bcom ldan 'das kyi phyag gnyis su gnas so//de nas rdo rje ral gri'i dbyibs de las 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rdul shin 
15a1(1): @#//tu phra mo dang mnyam pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku rnams byung nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi shes rab kyi ye shes la sogs pa dang/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi 
15a2(1): rdzu 'phrul dang cho 'phrul rnams bya ste/rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni shin tu 'jam pa'i dpal (la)ga yin pa dang/shin tu brtan pa yin pa'i phyir gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas/byang chub sems   
15a3(1): dpa' chen po 'jam dpal gzhon nu'i skur gyur ste/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs la gnas nas/ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//e ma'o sangs rgyas thams cad 
15a4(1): kyi|'jam dbyangs bdag ni yin par dgongs/gang phyir shes rab gzugs med kyang//dbyangs nyid du ni dmigs pa yin//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chen po 'jam dpal gyi sku 
15a5(1): de bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs las byung ste/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyis g.yas phyogs kyi zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas nas yang bka' nod cing bzhugs par gyur te//
15a6(1): de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi shes rab kyi rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi nyon mongs pa 
15a7(1): thams cad gcad pa'i dam tshig dang/sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa'i sdug bsngal thams cad bcad pa dang/thams cad kyi bde ba dang yid bde ba myong bar

15b1(1): chub sems dpa' chen po 'jam dpal gyi lag tu de bzhin gshedu byin no//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rdo rje'i blo zhes bya ba'i rdo rje ming gi dbang gis dbang bskur ro//de nas byang

15b5(1): tshig ces bya bde bzhin gshegs pa thams pa cad kyi snying po 'di rang gi thugs ka nas phyung ngo//badzra he du/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi thugs las byung ma thag pa 'di ni/bcom ldan 
15b6(1): 'das rdo rje 'dzin pa de nyid de rdo rje dbyings kyi dkyil 'khor chen po la sogs pa/de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad kyi dkyil 'khor du gyur te byung nas/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi 
15b7(1): thugs su zhugs te/gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas/rdo rje 'khor lo'i dbyibs su gyur te/bcom ldan 'das kyi phyag gnyis su gnas par gyur to//de nas rdo rje 'khor lo'i dbyibs de las 'jig

16a1(1): @#/ O /rnams kyi rdzu 'phrul gyi cho 'phrul rnams 'jig rten gyi khams (thams)cad du byas te/rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni sems bskyed ma thag tu chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba yin pa dang/shin

16a4(1): de nas byang chub sems dpa' chen pa sems bskyed ma thag tu chog kyi 'khor bskor ba'i sku de/bcom ldan 'das kyi thug Ilas byung ste/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi g.yon phyogs kyi zla
